## Narration 50

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-abudawud%3A2134)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 12</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنَا مُوسَى بْنُ إِسْمَاعِيلَ، حَدَّثَنَا حَمَّادٌ، عَنْ أَيُّوبَ، عَنْ أَبِي قِلاَبَةَ، عَنْ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ يَزِيدَ الْخَطْمِيِّ، عَنْ عَائِشَةَ، قَالَتْ كَانَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم يَقْسِمُ فَيَعْدِلُ وَيَقُولُ ‏ &quot;‏ اللَّهُمَّ هَذَا قَسْمِي فِيمَا أَمْلِكُ فَلاَ تَلُمْنِي فِيمَا تَمْلِكُ وَلاَ أَمْلِكُ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏ يَعْنِي الْقَلْبَ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 2134]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/2134" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Abi Dawud</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Sunan Abi Dawud · 2134</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Sunan Abi Dawud   2134 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p><a href="https://github.com/fawazahmed0/hadith-api">Fawaz Ahmed Hadith API</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Narrated Aisha, Ummul Mu&#x27;minin: The Messenger of Allah (ﷺ) used to divide his time equally and said: O Allah, this is my division concerning what I control, so do not blame me concerning what You control and I do not. Abu Dawud said: By it meant the heart</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 2134]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/2134" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Abi Dawud</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Sunan Abi Dawud · 2134</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Sunan Abi Dawud   2134 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p><a href="https://github.com/fawazahmed0/hadith-api">Fawaz Ahmed Hadith API</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">उम्म-उल-मोमिनीन आइशा (रदी अल्लाहू अन्हा) कहती हैं कि रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) (अपनी बीवियों की) बारी मुक़र्रर फ़रमाते थे और इस में इंसाफ़ से काम लेते थे, फिर ये दुआ फ़रमाते थे: &#x27;ऐ अल्लाह! ये मेरी तक़सीम उन चीज़ों में है जो मेरे बस में है, रही वो बात जो मेरे बस से बाहर है और तेरे बस में है (यानी दिल का मैलान) तो तू मुझे इसकी वजह से मलामत न फ़रमाना।&#x27;</p>

<p dir="auto">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 2134]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/2134" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Abi Dawud</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Hindi · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Sunan Abi Dawud · 2134</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Sunan Abi Dawud   2134 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p><a href="https://github.com/fawazahmed0/hadith-api">Fawaz Ahmed Hadith API</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">ام المؤمنین عائشہ رضی اللہ عنہا کہتی ہیں کہ رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم ( اپنی بیویوں کی ) باری مقرر فرماتے تھے اور اس میں انصاف سے کام لیتے تھے، پھر یہ دعا فرماتے تھے: اے اللہ! یہ میری تقسیم ان چیزوں میں ہے جو میرے بس میں ہے، رہی وہ بات جو میرے بس سے باہر ہے اور تیرے بس میں ہے ( یعنی دل کا میلان ) تو تو مجھے اس کی وجہ سے ملامت نہ فرمانا ۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 2134]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/2134" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Abi Dawud</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<summary>Urdu · references (1)</summary>

<p dir="auto">[ Sunan Abi Dawud   2134 ]</p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Umm-ul-Momineen Aisha (Radi Allahu Anha) kehti hain ki Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) (apni biwiyon ki) baari muqarrar farmate the aur is mein insaaf se kaam lete the, phir ye dua farmate the: &#x27;Aye Allah! Ye meri taqseem un cheezon mein hai jo mere bas mein hai, rahi woh baat jo mere bas se bahar hai aur Tere bas mein hai (yani dil ka mailan) to Tu mujhe iski wajah se malamat na farmana.&#x27;</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 2134]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/2134" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Abi Dawud</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>
